The list starts with the 2.0 launch on August 17, 2026. ​ 2026-10-28 Milian Automations Assistants Academy Missions Tools Telephony Flow Builder API History Mobile October 28, 2026 ​ Clickable answer buttons in live chat In website chat and Test Chat, an assistant can now show 2–5 clickable answer buttons under its message when the visitor is choosing among a few known options. Until now, every answer had to be typed. Off until you turn it on: in the assistant’s Settings → Conversation → Live chat → Interactive responses , switch on Quick replies . The When to use them (optional) field tells the assistant when buttons help. A click is a normal message: the visitor can always type instead. Phone calls, web voice, WhatsApp and other messaging, and email ignore the setting. It is not the same as the quick-reply buttons on a WhatsApp template. In History: the conversation shows the tool call as Show quick replies , and a click appears as the visitor’s message. Quick replies · Interactive responses ​ Separate transfer hours for each transfer Call transfers now support custom transfer hours. Until now, a transfer tool could connect at any time — the only hours the assistant knew were the general opening hours from the Business hours tool. You can now define separate timeframes for when call transfers are allowed. For example, your business is open from 8 AM to 6 PM, but accounting only takes calls until 3 PM. Set it per tool: turn on Restrict transfer hours on a call transfer or warm call transfer tool and add up to four windows per weekday. Night services such as 18:00–08:00 work too, and each tool can use its own timezone. No dead ends: outside the hours nothing is dialed. The assistant learns when the next transfer is possible and is instructed to tell the caller and to offer to take a message or arrange a callback; the conversation continues. In flows, a refused transfer never ends the call. Nothing changes until you turn it on: existing transfer tools keep transferring at any time. Each refused transfer is recorded as a call event. Transfer hours are also available through the API ( PATCH /api/v1/tools/{id} ), MCP ( update_tool ) and the command line ( famulor update-tool ). Transfer hours ​ Choose how deeply Milian thinks A quick question and a full campaign analysis no longer get the same amount of thinking. Milian now has four thinking levels: Low , Medium , High and Extra high . In the composer: the gauge button in the Milian composer opens Thinking level . Drag the slider or use the arrow keys. Medium is the default, and Milian remembers your choice in this browser. This works on the dashboard and in the side panel. For missions: in Mission Studio , the Access tab has a Thinking level select under Limits . Every run of that mission uses it. Default follows the platform default. Credits: higher levels think longer and can use more credits. You pay for actual usage, as before. Through the Public API and the ask_milian MCP tool, send effort ( low , medium , high or xhigh ). Missions take the same field in their version spec . Ask Milian through the API ​ Folders, templates and share links for automations Setting up the same follow-up for every client, or sending a colleague screenshots of your steps? Automations now have folders, a template gallery and share links. Until now, every automation sat in one long list and started from an empty canvas. Folders: choose New → New folder and move automations with Move to folder… in the ⋯ menu, for example one folder per client or location. Deleting a folder never deletes its automations. 41 templates: New → Start from template opens ready-made automations for calls, bookings, leads, messaging, CRM and team alerts. Use template creates a draft and marks every step that still needs your own account, number, assistant or recipient. Share without keys: Share… in the ⋯ menu creates a link. Anyone who opens it sees the steps and can add a copy to their own workspace. Connections, API keys, passwords, phone numbers and assistants never travel with it. Text you typed into steps, such as prompts or email addresses, stays, so check it before you share. Update link shows your latest version under the same link, and Stop sharing turns it off at once. Export and import: Export downloads a .famulor-automation.json file, and Import next to New turns one into a draft, in this or another workspace. On or off from the list: every row has a switch for Live and Paused . An automation goes live only once its required fields are filled in and placeholders such as YOUR_API_KEY are replaced. New connection right there: the Connections tab has its own New connection button. Booking triggers now also pass flat fields such as invitee_name , invitee_email , invitee_phone , start_at and end_at , so a booking automation can greet the guest by name without digging through nested data. Folders, templates, share links, export and import also work through the API, MCP, the command line ( famulor 0.1.12), SDK 0.2.7, the n8n node 2.4.0 and the Famulor Skill 2.4.0. Update your n8n node and the Skill to get the new actions. Folders, templates and sharing ​ Waiting automations no longer hold up new events An automation that waits in a delay step, for example 30 minutes before a reminder, no longer blocks the next events. Until now, three waiting runs were enough for the next booking or call to be turned away. Waiting isn’t working: up to three runs per automation can still work at the same time, and up to 200 more can wait. Paused stays paused: if you pause, archive or delete an automation while runs are waiting, they stop when they wake up and send nothing. The run history shows them as cancelled. There’s nothing to set up. A delay still waits at most one day. ​ Transcripts in the order things were said After an interruption or a handoff to another assistant, a call transcript could list lines in the wrong order. For example, a caller confirms their details, the assistant checks them and hands the call to a second assistant, and that assistant’s first sentence showed up before the caller’s last words, although it was said seconds later. This affected the API, webhooks, automations, the mobile app and some calls in History . One order, everywhere: every line, tool call and tool result is listed at the moment it started. When the caller talks while the assistant is speaking, whatever started first comes first. Typed chats keep the order the messages were sent. Older calls too: calls from before this change get the same order when you open or fetch them. Their content stays the same; only the order is corrected. The same list in every place: History, the mobile app, the API, MCP, webhooks and automations now show a call the same way. One card per tool in History: a tool that reports progress while it runs now appears as one card with its progress steps instead of several cards. Times in History now show when each line or step started, so a few can differ slightly from before. For developers: call transcripts in the API and in webhooks carry order_version: 2 , timeline_seq is an item’s position and started_at its start time. Saved Zaps and n8n workflows keep working without changes. In the mobile app, call transcripts now match History, with a time on every line. Update to version 1.5.4 from the App Store or Google Play to get this. Conversation history · Post-call webhooks ​ QA scorecards: no more 0 % for silent calls, and the judge sees what the assistant did Until now, a call in which the caller never said a word got 0 % and Failed on its QA scorecard, for example when someone hung up silently right after the greeting. And the QA judge only read what was said: a criterion like “Transfers only during service hours” could score 0, even though the assistant had checked the time with a tool and then transferred the caller. Not scorable instead of 0 %: calls in which the caller never spoke, that never connected, or whose speech couldn’t be transcribed now show Not scorable with the reason. They count neither as passed nor as failed and no longer pull down QA averages, pass rates or the Audience Call QA average. The judge sees actions: tool calls and their results, handoffs, transfers and how the call ended are now part of what the judge reads, in phone calls, web calls, WhatsApp calls and web chat. The caller never hears these lines. Criteria that don’t apply: the judge can mark a criterion Not applicable , for example a callback criterion in a call that was transferred right away. It is left out of the overall score instead of counting as 0. Outages stay visible: if the QA judge fails, the call shows QA judge unavailable instead of a silent 0 %. Find these calls: they no longer appear under QA result → Failed . Filter History by QA result → Not scorable , or use GET /api/v1/history?qa=not_scorable , the MCP tool list_history or famulor list-history --qa not_scorable . Scores can change from today: criteria that depend on actions, such as transfers, bookings or checks, may now score higher, because the judge sees that the assistant carried them out. In the API, MCP and webhooks, qa_scorecard now has status and reason , and score and passed are null when a call wasn’t scored. If an automation branches on passed , treat null as “no result”. In AI Quality Assurance cohorts, calls without caller speech are listed as No caller speech : they are not judged, not charged and not counted in totals or averages. AI QA scorecards · AI Quality Assurance ​ Also improved today ​ Long tool and calendar lists stay readable In an assistant’s settings, every assigned tool and calendar now has its own full row, and Available in your workspace and Available calendars stay at the bottom while the list above scrolls. Calendar and Notifications have their own menu items, and tags get a soft color of their own, the same on the assistant list, in the tag filter and in automations. ​ Academy lessons on every screen Lessons now fit phones, tablets, laptops and large monitors in English, German, French and Spanish. Words no longer break letter by letter, and switches and buttons keep their size. ​ More room for mission instructions In Mission Studio, the Instructions field on the Overview tab now fills the drawer, so long instructions are readable without scrolling inside the field. ​ 2026-10-27 Integrations Assistants Voice October 27, 2026 ​ Cal.com bookings in your caller’s language, at the place they choose When an assistant books through Cal.com, your guest’s confirmation email now arrives in the language of the conversation. Until now, Cal.com sent it in English, even when the assistant spoke German or French. Right language: the email follows the language the assistant speaks in the call or chat, otherwise its primary language. There’s nothing to set up. Right place: if your event type offers several locations, for example Google Meet and a phone call, the assistant books the one the caller asks for. Without a preference, Cal.com’s default location applies as before. Ask first, if you want: turn on Ask callers which location they prefer under Meeting location in the Cal.com integration. When the event type offers at least two locations, the assistant then asks before booking, unless the caller already said where; without a preference it books the default. Always current: the locations come straight from your Cal.com event type. Change them in Cal.com and the next conversation uses them, without editing the integration. This works on phone and web calls, WhatsApp calls, web chat, messaging channels and email. Calendar booking ​ Announced transfers go through, exactly once When an assistant tells a caller it is connecting them, the transfer now really follows, and only once. Until now, an assistant could announce a transfer without starting it, or start it a second time after the caller was already through. Announced means started: if the assistant says “I’m connecting you now” but doesn’t start the transfer, Famulor starts it right away. Announced texts, emails and hang-ups work the same way. A check beforehand, for example of the time against your service hours, no longer gets in the way. Offers stay offers: “If you like, I can connect you with a colleague” leaves the choice to the caller and starts nothing. Quiet after the transfer: once a transfer has gone through, the assistant says nothing more and never transfers a second time, on every transfer path, including fallbacks and transfer nodes in flows. There’s nothing to set up. Announcements are recognized in German and English conversations. Built-in tools ​ Also improved today ​ Plans and lists on your phone On a phone, Settings → Plan now shows one card per plan with its price and button, and the features to compare below. Page actions that wrap under the title stay on the right, list rows show full names and numbers, (i) icons are easier to tap, and the Live monitoring button on a running call fits again. ​ New guide: OpenScape Business (Mitel) Send calls from an OpenScape Business X1, X3, X5, X8 or S to your assistant. The guide walks through the settings in the PBX and explains why it needs a fixed public IP: without registration, it sends no password. PBX and contact center platforms ​ 2026-10-24 Channels History Assistants Automations Billing Usage October 24, 2026 ​ Your assistant, in more places The same assistant can now answer in ten more places: Linear , Notion , Mattermost , Webex , Lark / Feishu , LINE , Twitch , Sendblue , Linq and Zernio . Connect a place: open Settings → Channels , choose the channel and follow its setup guide. The channel is available when your plan includes it. Stay in the thread: when someone @mentions the assistant in Slack, Teams, Discord, Google Chat, Linear, Notion, Twitch, Mattermost, Webex or Lark, later replies in that same thread still reach the assistant. Reply from History: Instagram and Messenger stay inside the 24-hour reply window. In Mattermost, Webex, Linq and Notion you can send one file back in the thread, up to 3 MB. Each new channel, and the channels you already use, has its own guide: what it is, why an assistant belongs there, and how to connect it. Open Channels · Set up Linear · Set up LINE ​ Duplicate an automation instead of rebuilding it Need the same follow-up flow for a second customer or location? Choose Duplicate in the automation list or in the editor. Until now, you had to rebuild every step by hand. Everything comes along: the copy is called “Name (Copy)” and keeps every step, trigger, setting, tag and the error notification. Starts as a draft: the copy stays off until you turn it on, so a live automation never runs twice. Its own connections: inbound webhooks get a new secret, and the copy gets its own app-event subscription when you turn it on. If an assistant should start the copy, connect it yourself. When the original runs after an assistant’s calls, keep the copy as a draft until you decide which one stays connected. Duplicating also works through the API ( POST /api/v1/automations/{id}/duplicate ), MCP ( duplicate_automation ), the command line ( famulor duplicate-automation ) and SDK 0.2.6. Automations overview ​ New credit rates for messages and email From October 24, messages in WhatsApp and messaging channels, workspace emails and web chat use new base credit rates. Calls, SMS and all other rates stay the same. Your workspace’s own rates are always listed under Usage → How credits are calculated and in Settings → Balance → How billing works . Both now show every rate for voice, avatars, WhatsApp, messaging channels, web chat, email and SMS, and name the channels each rate covers. On Usage , Credits by channel now also breaks each category down by channel. How usage is billed ​ Also improved today ​ Bot names that match your domain Partners on their own domain no longer see “famulor-bot” as the example bot name when they connect Slack, Linear, Mattermost, Webex, Lark, LINE or Zernio. The example now uses your domain’s name, for example “yourbrand-bot”. Saved connections stay as they are. ​ 2026-10-23 Tools Integrations Automations Whitelabel Billing October 23, 2026 ​ Your MCP servers, on every call You connected an MCP server, for example from HubSpot or Salesforce, and Load tools worked, but in calls the assistant didn’t use those tools? Some servers whose address doesn’t end in /mcp were left out when a call started, and the call went on without them. They now connect on phone calls, web calls, web chat and WhatsApp calls. Clear token errors: when a server rejects your token, Load tools now says Token rejected or expired and tells you what to do next. Before, it looked like the server couldn’t be reached. Short-lived tokens: some providers, such as Salesforce, issue access tokens that expire after a few hours. If tools stop loading, paste a new token. Agent Tools ​ More HighLevel and Close options Agencies can now find three more connectors in the app marketplace under Automations → Connections : HighLevel Location with more than 550 actions, HighLevel MCP and Close MCP . They sit next to the native HighLevel and Close integrations, which stay as they are. ​ For partners: managed plans and reseller billing Managed plans renew or end clearly: Auto top-up, then renew is preselected and recommended when auto top-up with a saved card is on. If a renewal is still unfunded three days after the funded period ends, the managed plan ends: unused funded credits return to your balance and the customer moves to your free plan. The dialog shows Renewal pending or Ending with the date and the missing credits. Plan changes charge the right fee: upgrades and add-ons added mid-period on resold subscriptions now collect the full platform fee up front. A change that can’t be charged correctly is refused before anything is billed. Before, a paid change could leave the customer workspace past due. Credits stay covered: a credit transfer can’t move credits that running calls have reserved. Clearer numbers: the plan limit for own numbers is now called Verified own numbers (caller ID) . Plan-included free phone numbers are not available to white-label customers. Phone numbers and SMS registrations are not part of a managed plan. Lighter plan editor: explanations sit in (i) tooltips, each pricing problem shows under its field, and one bar splits the plan price into platform fee, payment reserve and your share. Your brand during outages: if your custom domain can’t load its branding, visitors see a neutral maintenance screen instead of Famulor branding. Managed customer plans ​ Also improved today ​ US privacy opt-out in Preferences For visitors from US states with privacy laws, the Do Not Sell or Share link no longer covers content on signed-in pages. It is now a button in Settings → Preferences , next to Cookie settings. Login, booking and other public pages keep the link. ​ 2026-10-21 Assistants Channels Email Magicline Tools Settings October 21, 2026 ​ Hand a chat or email to the right assistant Transfer to another assistant used to work only on calls and in web chat. Now it also works in WhatsApp, SMS, Instagram, Slack, connected mailboxes and every other messaging channel, and in assistant email. For example, your support assistant hands an invoice question to your billing assistant, and the customer gets the answer in the same thread. Same thread, own setup: the receiving assistant answers in the same reply, with its own instructions, knowledge and tools, and keeps answering the customer’s next messages. The channel’s default assistant doesn’t change. You decide what it knows: choose the same context as on calls: the full history, a summary plus the last messages (recommended), only the last messages, a summary or a clean start. A message before the handoff and the target’s greeting are optional. Safe handoffs: actions the first assistant started finish before the handoff. Human takeover, recipient checks and the protection against duplicate emails and texts still apply. Transfer to another assistant ​ Magicline Connect: book trial sessions in the conversation “Can I try a class on Friday?” Your assistant can now book the trial session itself. The new Magicline Connect API connector finds your studios, checks free times and books once the visitor has confirmed. You don’t need a Magicline API key. The existing connector is now called Magicline Open API and keeps reading studio and course data. Find your studios: open Agent Tools → Marketplace → Magicline Connect API , enter your Connect API URL and choose Load studios . Search by name or city and pick one studio or All studios in this account . Studio IDs are filled in for you. Book only after a yes: the assistant confirms the studio, reads the required fields and free times, collects the details and asks the visitor to confirm before it books. It says “booked” only after Magicline confirms. Repeated or parallel requests don’t create double bookings. Trainer preference: choose Studio default , With a trainer or Without a trainer , if your studio allows it. A specific trainer can’t be picked. In automations too: connect Magicline Connect API under Automations → Connections and use its requirements, free times and booking actions. Your studio needs Connect API enabled, and online trial sessions for bookings. Free times are not reservations: if a time fills up, the assistant loads new times. Setup also works through the API, MCP and the command line. Set up Magicline Connect · Magicline Open API ​ Also new today ​ Sign in with Google, Apple, GitHub or LinkedIn Open Settings → Preferences → Sign-in methods and connect a Google, Apple, GitHub or LinkedIn account to sign in with one click. Your email and password keep working, and the last sign-in method can’t be disconnected. Available in the Famulor web app, not on white-label domains. Sign-in methods ​ 2026-10-20 Models Voice Assistants Integrations API October 20, 2026 ​ Grok Voice 3.0: a new voice for your assistants Your assistant can now hold spoken conversations with Grok Voice 3.0 . Native speech-to-speech brings listening and expressive speech into one conversation model, with 28 public voices to choose from. Choose your voice: open Assistant settings → General → Realtime → Standard , select Grok Voice 3.0 , then choose a compatible voice. Eve is the recommended starting point; Rex and Ara are other options. Preview a voice before saving. Connect the next step: use your assistant’s assigned tools to look up information or continue a workflow during the conversation. Follow the same tool permissions and configuration as your other assistants. Make it useful: try customer questions, appointment intake, language practice or initial sales conversations. Test the assistant with your own prompts, tools and callers. Manual model selection requires AI Safety & Control . Grok Voice is available in Global and US workspaces and has no dedicated EU endpoint . Use Realtime → Standard ; Half-Cascade and Full Duplex are separate engine choices. A Famulor voice agent running on Grok Voice 3.0. Choose models and voices · Check AI regions ​ Signed webhooks for your own apps Send call, conversation and booking events straight to your own HTTPS receiver. A workspace owner or admin registers the receiver and picks its events: call.completed , conversation.ended , booking.created , booking.cancelled and booking.rescheduled . Signed deliveries: every event is signed with HMAC-SHA256 in the X-Famulor-Signature header. Your signing secret stays with your receiver and is never shown again. Wherever you build: create, list and delete subscriptions through the API, MCP, the command line and the SDK. n8n sets itself up: with n8n-nodes-famulor 2.3.0 or later, a new Famulor Trigger registers its subscription when you activate the workflow and removes it when you deactivate it. Existing workflows keep their manual setup. Update the package in n8n to use it. Zapier templates: the Zapier page now shows ready-made Zap templates you can start from. Set up webhook subscriptions · n8n · Zapier ​ Also new today ​ Talk to Famulor live The live demo gallery lets anyone talk to Famulor agents in the browser, from a hotel front desk and order support to lead qualification and candidate screening. Compare engines from Pipeline to Realtime and Full Duplex. Demos start in the language of the page. Share it with your team or your customers before you build. ​ Transcripts that appear as they are spoken Live transcripts in the website widget, test calls and test chats now reveal each word with a soft fade, in time with the speech. If you have reduced motion turned on, text appears right away. ​ 2026-10-19 Magicline Tools Automations Simulations October 19, 2026 ​ Magicline: your studio, in the conversation “Which classes are on tomorrow?” “Is there still a place?” Your assistant can now look up studio information, courses and availability through Magicline , while the visitor stays in the conversation. Connect your studio under Agent Tools → Marketplace → Magicline , enter its Studio ID, API key and Magicline Open API URL, then assign the connection to an assistant. Existing AI-Fitness proxy connections remain supported. In Edit → Tools , choose exactly which functions the assistant can use. Answer from the connected studio: six read functions cover studio details, the course list, course descriptions, schedules, course dates and individual slot availability. Support more than one location: connect each studio separately. Studio-specific tool names let one assistant use the right connection after clarifying the location. Build the next step: under Automations → Connections , connect Magicline or import an Agent Tool connection. Use a Magicline action in a flow with your other connected apps. Simple assistant lookups do not require an automation. The broader operation catalog helps you configure additional reads according to your provider permissions. Booking and other write operations are not available yet. A free place or a bookable course is availability information, not a confirmed reservation. Connect Magicline · Choose assistant tools ​ Simulations that use your assigned tools Chat simulations now discover assigned tool functions and their input fields. External actions return clearly simulated responses, so follow-up steps can use consistent example IDs without creating real bookings or CRM entries. Knowledge retrieval and the current-time tool remain real. The same runner powers individual tests and Run all . Test your assistant ​ 2026-10-18 Widgets Automations Personio Channels Tools Milian October 18, 2026 ​ Greet website visitors before they click Your website widget can now say hello first. Under Appearance → Launcher , turn on Greeting bubble . Your greeting then appears next to the closed launcher when a visitor hovers over it or reaches it with the keyboard, and a click on the bubble opens the widget. Show it on its own: under Also show automatically , choose Desktop , Mobile or both, and set how many seconds (0–60) after loading the bubble appears. It shows at most once per browser tab, waits while the tab is in the background and stays hidden once the visitor has opened the widget. Write once, greet in every language: under Messages → Chat , write your Greeting message , pick the language you wrote it in and keep Auto-translate on. When you save, the headline and greeting are translated into your assistant’s languages, up to 40. Visitors see them in their browser language, or in the assistant’s main language. Stay in control: the editor shows which languages are translated, and Retry translation fills in any that are missing. New widgets start with Auto-translate on. Existing widgets keep their greeting as it is until you turn it on. The greeting bubble works with the classic floating launcher. Inline widgets and full-screen avatar widgets don’t show it. Set up the greeting bubble · Translate your greeting ​ New app integration: Personio Connect Personio to Automations and use your HR data in workflows: people, employments, absences, attendances, time-tracking projects and documents. For example, check who is absent today, or pull a person’s employment details into a follow-up workflow. Personio has no sign-in window for other apps, so you connect it with your own Personio API credential. Create the credential in Personio under Marketplace → Connected integrations → API credentials with read access to persons. Then open Automations → Connections → App marketplace → Personio and paste its Client ID and secret. Famulor checks the credential right away. It stays at the app connector and is never stored in your workspace. Ten read actions: list persons, employments, absence periods and types, attendance periods, projects, documents and document categories, employee attributes and employee records. Replace credentials any time: edit the connection and paste a new pair. The old credential is removed in the same step. Milian knows the way: ask Milian to connect Personio and it points you to the form, because credentials never go into the chat. Connect Personio ​ Emails and texts that reach the right person, once When your assistant sends an email or a text message during a chat, an email conversation or a call, it now makes sure the recipient is right, and it sends each message only once. Confirmed email recipients: with Ask on the call as the recipient, the assistant asks for the address or reads back the one it knows, and sends only after the customer confirms it. In chats, the address must come from the customer, never from a web page or a tool result. Texts to the right number: in SMS and WhatsApp chats, texts always go to the chat’s own number. In other chats, the assistant only texts a number the customer wrote. No duplicates: if a chat or email reply is generated again, an email or text that already went out is not sent a second time. When the customer asks for it again, it goes out again. Numbers as people write them: texts and callbacks accept numbers such as +49 176 …, 0049 … or WhatsApp numbers without a plus. When a number has no country code, the assistant asks for it instead of guessing. No gaps in your emails: placeholders in custom email signatures, such as the assistant’s name, are filled in. If a required value is missing, the email is not sent. Calls stay on track: calls no longer go silent when the AI model sends a malformed tool request, for example while asking for an email address. Confirming a collected email address, name or phone number now works on the first “yes” with Realtime, Half-Cascade and Full Duplex too. How the SMS and email tools work ​ Also improved today ​ Callbacks and availability on the customer’s local day Callbacks scheduled from a chat or an email now use your assistant’s time zone, so a callback promised for 2:30 pm happens at 2:30 pm. Leaving Max days ahead empty now means no limit, as it does on calls. Calendly and eTermin availability checks in chats and emails cover exactly the day the customer asked for. Set up callbacks ​ Replies that keep going Chat replies, email replies and Milian now keep working when the AI model sends an incomplete tool request. A connected MCP server that doesn’t answer is stopped after the tool’s timeout, so the reply can continue. In Zoho Mail inboxes, the assistant can now also look up recent emails with the same customer without keywords. ​ See tools your plan doesn’t include yet The App Store under Agent Tools now shows tools such as Browser Use and Recall Request even when your plan doesn’t include them. They carry an Upgrade badge; open one to see which plans unlock it. Explore Agent Tools ​ Clearer tool dialogs Every add, connect and edit dialog in Agent Tools now follows one layout: app logo, name and a one-line description at the top, with longer help behind the (i) icon. In Browser Use , allowed websites are now chips you can add and remove, and entering the start page suggests its website with and without “www”. Set up Browser Use ​ Milian suggests Browser Use when it fits Describe the job, for example checking room prices on a booking website during calls, and Milian suggests Browser Use when no app integration, API or MCP tool, or knowledge source fits better. Ask for Browser Use directly and Milian sets it up for you. Work with Milian ​ A tidier device list Under Settings → Preferences → Where you are signed in , sessions are now grouped by device, with your current session first. Sign out a whole device at once, or choose Sign out other sessions to stay signed in only where you are. Review your devices ​ API keys without sideways scrolling Settings → API Keys now fits the screen. Long key names are shortened, Revoke and Delete always sit at the end of the row, and revoked keys are marked under their name. ​ More details in the signup webhook For white-label partners, the signup webhook now also sends the sign-up language, first and last name, and the country the customer signed up from, when known. Set up the signup webhook ​ A cleaner App Catalog The App Catalog now lists only apps you can connect from your workspace. Apps that Famulor connects natively, such as HighLevel, no longer appear a second time. Browse the App Catalog ​ 2026-10-17 Browser Use Assistants History October 17, 2026 ​ Give your assistant a browser Your assistant can now work with approved websites during a conversation. Create a Browser Use tool in Agent Tools , choose a starting page and allowed websites, and assign it to an assistant. Use it to look up information or complete supported website steps with the details provided in the conversation. Start with practical lookups: Compare visible flight fares on Google Flights, hotel prices, appointment availability, product stock or shipment status. The guide includes starting pages, required information and example browser instructions. Test the selected website flow before using it with customers. Configure the task once: Save Browser instructions and Required information directly in the tool, then assign it to an assistant. The assistant asks for missing information before opening the browser; the main assistant prompt does not need to repeat the browser steps. Recordings are private to the workspace and follow its retention settings, up to seven days. Control access and usage: Browser Use is available to Famulor workspaces on enabled plans, including a whitelabel reseller’s own workspace. Browser tasks use separate credits, shown as Browser Use in usage history. Reseller customer workspaces are not included yet. Phone, website-widget and WhatsApp conversations keep their familiar interface. The browser tool runs when the assistant needs it; the live viewer is for testing and enabled public demos. The assistant can explain visible content, but cannot click or control the device. System and tab audio are not shared, and screenshots are processed on demand without being saved as attachments. Voice mode keeps a compact input row and toolbar: page context, screen sharing and microphone controls are icon toggles. The eye icon pauses or resumes page context and dims while screen sharing is active. New voice messages also arrive without a flashing scrollbar. Enable Beta Features in your workspace to use Milian voice. Learn about Milian voice and screen help ​ What you can use it for Customer support: Visitors can show an error message or the step where they are stuck. The assistant can explain what is visible and suggest the next step. Onboarding: New users can share a setup page and ask where to find a setting or how to complete a step, without describing the whole screen. Shopping assistance: Customers can show a product page or comparison table and ask the assistant to explain the visible options, alongside information from your knowledge base. Booking help: Visitors can share a booking page and ask for help understanding the available dates or completing the next step themselves. 2026-10-01 Knowledge API CLI October 1, 2026 ​ Better answers from your website Website imports now keep the content your assistants need and leave out what gets in the way. No more cookie banners or menus in your knowledge base. Consent banners and dialogs, navigation and footers are removed before a page is indexed; prices, opening hours, allergens and other details stay. Every passage knows where it belongs. Pages are split along their headings, and each passage starts with the page title and section name, for example Pizza Hawaii › Ingredients . Assistants find the ingredient list, allergens or baking time of the right product instead of a similar one. Less noise. Error pages, pages without readable content and pages with identical content are skipped and use no credits. An error page never replaces a page that was indexed before. Existing website sources pick up the new cleaning at their next refresh. This one-time re-index is free. Pages imported earlier that are now skipped stay in your knowledge base until you delete them. ​ The same structure for files and texts PDFs, Word files, Markdown and text files, typed text and HTML files are now prepared like websites: Every passage starts with the document name , so even a passage from the middle of a catalog says which document it comes from. Word and Markdown headings split a document into sections , for example Speisekarte › Pizza Hawaii › Zutaten . PDF page numbers disappear , and headers or footers that repeat on almost every page are kept only once. HTML files lose their cookie banners , like websites. Documents already in your knowledge bases are converted once automatically, free of charge. How content is prepared ​ Choose exactly which pages to import Tick individual pages in the page preview. Each checkbox adds an entry for exactly that page (ending in $ , for example /impressum$ ); a path without $ still covers every page below it. The page limit follows your selection. Maximum pages rises automatically so every include path and ticked page fits, and you can type an exact number. The dialog and the Auto Refresh cards warn you when a source has more include paths than its page limit. Refresh less often when content rarely changes. Auto Refresh for websites and auto-sync for cloud drives now run from every 6 hours up to every 6 months. Privacy, cookie and accessibility pages are suggested as exclusions; the imprint and terms are never suggested. Import a website ​ Delete several documents at once Hover over a document and tick its checkbox, then use Select all or Delete selected in the bar above the list. Deleting also removes the indexed passages, so assistants stop using that content right away. API: DELETE /knowledge-bases/{id}/documents/{docId} for one document, POST /knowledge-bases/{id}/documents/delete for up to 200 MCP: delete_documents CLI: famulor delete-knowledge-base-document and famulor delete-knowledge-base-documents Delete documents ​ 2026-10-01 API CLI October 1, 2026 ​ The Famulor CLI Available as a public preview . Requires Node.js 22 or newer and a workspace plan with API Access . Command names may change before version 1.0. Your whole Famulor workspace is now available in the terminal. Every REST API operation is a command: create assistants, start calls, run campaigns, manage leads and phone numbers, and read call history, all from one tool. npm install --global famulor famulor auth login famulor list-assistants --output table Log in once with an API key from Settings → API & MCP . The key is stored in your system keychain, with one profile per workspace and support for white-label domains. Tables, JSON or JSON Lines , with --all to fetch every page of a list. Safe for scripts and CI : deletes and irreversible actions need confirmation, --dry-run shows a request before it is sent, rate limits are retried automatically, and in scripts a failure ends with one JSON error line on stderr. Made for coding agents : famulor commands <topic> finds the right command and famulor <command> --help --output json describes it, so Claude Code, Codex or Cursor can use Famulor directly. Daily reports follow the Mission timezone. Requests for the previous calendar day use the correct local-day boundaries, including daylight-saving changes. Conversation history stays in order. Your request appears before Milian’s tools and reply, tool activity follows the order it was started, and duplicate replies from older chats are filtered out. Stay in the conversation you selected. Opening an assistant or automation editor during a turn no longer switches the chat when Milian finishes. Background conversations no longer redirect the visible page. Clearer subagent setup. Before building a workflow with subagents, Milian asks whether callers should experience separate departments or one agent handling different tasks. The choice guides introductions and handoffs. For an assistant with additional languages, choose a compatible multilingual voice; the voice picker accounts for your assistant’s language configuration. ​ 2026-08-29 Assistants Telephony August 29, 2026 ​ Warm transfers can bring a message back to the caller A warm transfer no longer leaves the assistant guessing when the handoff doesn’t complete. If the supervisor answers but cannot take the call, they can say, for example, “Ask them to call back in an hour” and hang up. The assistant returns to the caller, briefly relays the relevant message in the caller’s language, and continues the conversation. When a warm transfer cannot be completed, the assistant can bring the supervisor's message back to the caller The outcome is always shared — the assistant receives the classified reason: busy, declined, no answer, or temporarily unavailable. It is explicitly told never to invent something the supervisor did not say. Consultation notes are your choice — Share supervisor conversation with the AI (consultation notes) controls whether the assistant sees the supervisor-side conversation when the supervisor actually spoke. The transcript is always saved to the call record, even when sharing is off. If nobody answers, there are no consultation notes — only the outcome. Every warm-transfer path behaves consistently — this works in Flow Builder warm transfers, the Warm transfer tool, and Famulor Loop Recall. Consultation notes are always enabled for Loop Recall because there is no separate transfer form there. ​ 2026-08-28 Assistants Automation August 28, 2026 ​ Create AI-native automations and run them during a conversation Create AI-native automations in Famulor, connect them to an assistant, and run them while the conversation is still going. The assistant can start a workflow during a phone call, web chat, messaging conversation, or email. You decide when it should run and which information it passes into the workflow. Capture leads, submit callback requests, create tasks, and run other business processes from the conversation itself. ​ Faster conversational voice responses Updated conversational voice options reduce the delay before an assistant starts speaking on phone and voice calls while retaining expressive delivery. Response time still depends on the selected voice and the conversation. Explore the available options in Models & voices . ​ 2026-08-26 Telephony August 26, 2026 ​ Famulor Loop, now in your browser Famulor Loop now has a Chrome side panel — your softphone, call history, and workspace switcher next to whatever tab you’re working in, plus notifications for incoming, missed, and failed calls and agent activity. Pair it to your workspace with a 4-digit PIN instead of a password; the Loop page has the setup steps. The Famulor Loop side panel, docked next to your browser tab ​ 2026-08-25 Billing Assistants August 25, 2026 ​ Milian offers to top up your credit, right in the chat If you run low on credit while chatting with Milian , it now shows an Add credits card right there in the conversation — no need to go find Settings → Balance yourself. ​ Assign a tool to several assistants at once Select Assign on any tool in Agent Tools to see and change which assistants use it — search the list, tick the ones you want, save. No more opening each assistant to add the same tool one at a time. ​ 2026-08-24 Assistants Automation API August 24, 2026 ​ Assistants can call an automation mid-conversation An assistant can now trigger an automation while it’s still talking to someone, not only after the conversation ends. Describe in plain language when it should run, switch that automation to Live, and the assistant decides in the moment to use it — across voice, web chat, messaging, and email. ​ Assistants can look up current information on the web A new Perplexity research connector in Agent Connectors lets an assistant fetch up-to-date answers during a call or a chat, beyond what’s already in its knowledge base. Install it with your own Perplexity API key, pick the model your account offers, and optionally set a system prompt for tone, language, and answer format — then assign the tool to as many assistants as you like. ​ Workspace-scoped API keys Issue a Public API key scoped to a single workspace , with its own permissions and expiry, instead of only account-wide keys. Agent Connectors — install a research tool like the new Perplexity connector ​ 2026-08-23 Assistants August 23, 2026 ​ Start from a ready-made assistant, not a blank page The Create assistant gallery now includes complete flow blueprints for common use cases, not just prompt templates. Each one shows what it’s built to do, which surfaces it works on, and anything you still need to finish before it goes live. See Start from a use-case template . ​ Teach an assistant your key terms Add up to 100 product names, brand terms, and other key vocabulary so speech recognition transcribes them correctly. Open Assistant settings → Voice → Speech recognition to build the glossary — it’s a plan feature, and the settings appear only for the engine modes and speech-recognition providers that support it. An optional Detect key terms automatically (Beta) toggle can pick up further terms for the current call. ​ 2026-08-22 Channels Assistants Automation August 22, 2026 ​ Ten new inboxes your assistant can answer in (Beta) Freshdesk, Gmail, Outlook, Zendesk, ServiceNow, Intercom, Zoho Mail, AgentMail, Instagram, and Zulip join WhatsApp, Telegram, Slack, and the rest of messaging — sign in to the account once and your assistant reads and replies automatically. Gmail and Outlook can watch the Inbox, the whole mailbox, or a single label or folder, and either send replies straight away or leave a draft for you to review. Turn on Beta Features under Settings → Workspace to see them. ​ Three more ways to keep your knowledge base current (Beta) Sync a knowledge base from Box, Dropbox, or SharePoint now too, alongside the existing Google Drive and OneDrive sources — kept up to date automatically, the same way a website source is. Cloud drive sync is Beta and carries its own plan feature. ​ Automations can now react to every message, not just the first An automation triggered by an inbound message used to fire only on the first one. It can now trigger on every inbound message , so a workflow can keep responding as the conversation continues. ​ 2026-08-21 Platform Automation August 21, 2026 ​ Milian is now AI-native Milian , the workspace copilot, can now do more than answer questions — it analyzes your assistants and calls, connects accounts and data sources, builds automations from scratch, and tests them before anything goes live. Most of what you used to click through by hand in the dashboard, you can now just ask Milian to do in chat. ​ Missions — recurring tasks in the background Milian Missions are scheduled tasks Milian runs for you on a recurring schedule or on demand, with no browser tab required to stay open. New Missions entry in the sidebar. ​ Rate Milian’s answers Hover over any Milian response to rate it with a thumbs up or down. After a helpful interaction, Milian may also nudge you to leave Famulor a review. ​ 2026-08-20 Assistants Settings August 20, 2026 ​ Test with a real phone call, not just text Simulation tests can now run as an actual voice call, not only a text conversation. A simulated caller — with its own voice from the voice library — calls your live, unmodified assistant, so you hear exactly what a real customer would experience before you ship a change. Two test modes: fast, text-based Chat , or full audio round-trip Voice . Every test now also scores turn-by-turn response latency and whether the assistant called the tools it was supposed to. ​ Workspace Audit Log — see who changed what Team accounts can now open Settings → Data → Audit Log for a complete history of changes across the workspace: who changed what, and when, down to the exact field. Searchable, sortable, and paginated. ​ Multiple workspaces per account You can now create more than one workspace under a single account — useful for keeping separate brands, clients, or departments cleanly apart without juggling logins. Availability depends on your plan. ​ 2026-08-19 Assistants Telephony August 19, 2026 ​ GoHighLevel joins the calendar-booking providers Your assistant can check availability and book, cancel, or reschedule appointments directly in GoHighLevel calendars — the same booking experience already available for Cal.com, Calendly, and other supported providers. ​ Call endings now match the assistant’s language When a call ends, the assistant’s closing line now speaks in the assistant’s own configured language instead of always defaulting to English. ​ More resilient voice calls Calls now recover more gracefully from a rare voice-provider hiccup instead of ending abruptly, keeping the conversation going for the caller. ​ 2026-08-18 Assistants Billing August 18, 2026 ​ Refer and Earn — get rewarded for every workspace you bring in Invite other businesses to Famulor and both sides get rewarded once the workspace you referred makes its first payment — up to 10 rewarded referrals per workspace. Copy your personal referral link from Settings → Workspace → Refer and earn ; rewards land automatically, no manual claiming. Currently available on Famulor-branded workspaces — see Refer and Earn . ​ A faster way to build and test assistants Squad view — see every hand-off route between transfer-linked assistants as a visual graph. Latency waterfall — a step-by-step breakdown of where time goes in a test call. Live tool progress — watch tool calls execute in real time instead of waiting for the result. One-click simulate from a real call — turn any past call straight into a repeatable test. ​ Customizable web widget launcher Pick your own launcher icon and a localized label for your web widget, with a roomier chat panel on desktop screens. ​ 2026-08-17 Platform Settings August 17, 2026 ​ Famulor 2.0 is live After a public beta, Famulor 2.0 is now live for everyone — a rebuilt platform with a faster, cleaner interface, and more capabilities landing since (see above). ​ Moving from Famulor 1.0? We bring your setup with you The built-in migration now carries over more of your existing setup automatically — your 1.0 folders and labels arrive as assistant tags, and your Gmail-send and Airtable automation actions are recreated as native Famulor 2.0 actions. Start it any time from Settings, or ask Milian to move things over for you. Looking for older releases? See the Famulor 1.0 changelog .
